   954	def _close_main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
   955	    """Plan 2026-05-11-002 v3 F004 — operator-resolved feature close-out.
   956	
   957	    Shape:
   958	      dontpanic close --operator-resolved <plan-id> <feature-id> --reason <class>
   959	
   960	    The ``--operator-resolved`` flag is required (the surface is reserved
   961	    for operator close-out of a ``stopped_no_progress`` terminal that the
   962	    operator judged non-defect). Other close shapes — plan-level
   963	    active → completed — live under ``dontpanic plan close``.
   964	    """
   965	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
   966	        prog="dontpanic close",
   967	        description=(
   968	            "Operator-resolved feature close-out for a stopped_no_progress "
   969	            "terminal that the operator judged non-defect. Generates a "
   970	            "minimal closeout-memo template, clears breaker:no_progress, "
   971	            "writes a signoff envelope, and flips features.json passes:true "
   972	            "for the feature in one transaction."
   973	        ),
   974	    )
   975	    parser.add_argument(
   976	        "--operator-resolved",
   977	        action="store_true",
   978	        dest="operator_resolved",
   979	        required=False,
   980	        help=(
   981	            "REQUIRED. Affirms the operator is closing out the feature "
   982	            "without a re-dispatch. Reserved flag — the surface refuses to "
   983	            "run without it so accidental close-outs don't slip through."
   984	        ),
   985	    )
   986	    parser.add_argument(
   987	        "plan",
   988	        help="Plan ID (resolved against ./docs/plans/) or absolute dir path.",
   989	    )
   990	    parser.add_argument(
   991	        "feature",
   992	        help="Feature ID — e.g. F001 — that hit stopped_no_progress.",
   993	    )
   994	    parser.add_argument(
   995	        "--reason",
   996	        required=True,
   997	        dest="reason_class",
   998	        metavar="CLASS",
   999	        help=(
  1000	            "Taxonomy class for the close-out (e.g. spec_ambiguity, "
  1001	            "scope_overreach, environmental_reproduction_failure, "
  1002	            "evidence_shape_disagreement, operator_judgment). Recorded in "
  1003	            "the signoff envelope's signoff_reason and on the closeout-memo "

  1005	        ),

  1010	        dest="allow_missing_breaker",
  1011	        help=(
  1012	            "Skip the safety check that refuses to close-out when "
  1013	            "breaker:no_progress is not currently active. Use only when "

  1015	        ),

  1026	    )

  1028	
  1029	    if not args.operator_resolved:
  1030	        print(

  1032	            "`dontpanic plan close <plan>` for plan-level lifecycle close.",
  1033	            file=sys.stderr,
  1034	        )
  1035	        return 2
  1036	
  1037	    plan_dir = _resolve_plan_dir(args.plan)
  1038	    loaded = plan_loader.load(plan_dir)
  1039

  1071	                tier=tier,
  1072	                agents_in_panel=agents,
  1073	                require_active_breaker=not args.allow_missing_breaker,
  1074	            )
  1075	    except closeout.CloseoutError as exc:

    51	def _next_action_for(volley_status: str, signed_off: bool) -> str:
    52	    if signed_off:
    53	        return "merge"
    54	    if volley_status == "blocked":
    55	        return "blocked_awaiting_human"
    56	    if volley_status == "paused_on_gate":
    57	        return "blocked_awaiting_human"
    58	    # Plan 2026-05-12-001 v4 F002 — auditor returned verdict=blocked with
    59	    # an empty findings list. Operator must inspect manually; we don't
    60	    # auto-promote to environmental because there's no advisory signal.
    61	    if volley_status == "blocked_no_findings":
    62	        return "blocked_awaiting_human"
    63	    # supervisor.dispatch_volley emits "stopped_cap" when iteration cap is
    64	    # reached; the legacy "stopped_max_iterations" alias is kept for callers
    65	    # that may emit it directly.
    66	    if volley_status in {
    67	        "stopped_quota",
    68	        "stopped_cap",
    69	        "stopped_max_iterations",
    70	        "stopped_no_progress",
    71	        "stopped_budget",
    72	        "stopped_wall_clock",
    73	        "stopped_diminishing_returns",
    74	        "stopped_convergence_collapse",
    75	        # Plan 2026-05-09-002 F003 — env-only first-round short-circuit.
    76	        # Operator runs the cited verification locally on a host with the
    77	        # missing capability, attaches evidence, then approves the breaker.
    78	        "stopped_environmental_blocker",
    79	    }:
    80	        return "remediate"
    81	    if volley_status == "stopped_global_breaker":
    82	        # Global circuit breaker is hard stop; operator can't approve, must
    83	        # wait. Closeout marks blocked_awaiting_human + signoff=false.
    84	        return "blocked_awaiting_human"
    85	    return "defer"

    96	def _summarize_votes(audit_paths: list[Path]) -> dict[str, int]:
    97	    counts = {
    98	        "signed_off_count": 0,
    99	        "needs_changes_count": 0,
   100	        "blocked_count": 0,
   101	        "inconclusive_count": 0,
   102	    }
   103	    for ap in audit_paths:
   104	        try:
   105	            data = json.loads(ap.read_text())
   106	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   107	            continue
   108	        status = data.get("audit_status")
   109	        key = f"{status}_count" if status else None
   110	        if key in counts:
   111	            counts[key] += 1
   112	    return counts
   113	
   114	
   115	def _quota_totals(audit_paths: list[Path]) -> dict[str, float]:
   116	    totals: dict[str, float] = {}
   117	    for ap in audit_paths:
   118	        try:
   119	            data = json.loads(ap.read_text())
   120	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   121	            continue
   122	        agent = data.get("agent")
   123	        qc = data.get("quota_consumed") or {}
   124	        pct = qc.get("percent_weekly")
   125	        if agent and isinstance(pct, (int, float)):
   126	            key = f"{agent}_percent"
   127	            totals[key] = max(totals.get(key, 0.0), float(pct))
   128	    # Filter to fields the schema knows about (claude_percent / codex_percent /
   129	    # gemini_percent only; grok_calls counted separately).
   130	    keep = {
   131	        k: v
   132	        for k, v in totals.items()
   133	        if k in {"claude_percent", "codex_percent", "gemini_percent"}
   134	    }
   135	    return keep

   663	
   664	def _latest_auditor_verdict(audit_paths: list[Path]) -> str | None:
   665	    """Return the latest auditor envelope's verdict string (``audit_status``),
   666	    or None when no auditor envelope is present."""
   667	    env = _latest_auditor_envelope(audit_paths)
   668	    if not isinstance(env, dict):
   669	        return None
   670	    status = env.get("audit_status")
   671	    return status if isinstance(status, str) else None
   672	
   673	
   674	def _recorded_gate_block(plan_dir: Path) -> str | None:
   675	    """Plan 2026-06-02-002 F002 — find a recorded downstream-gate block to use
   676	    as staging_blocked evidence. Returns a short evidence descriptor (the
   677	    artifact relpath) when a ``patch-completeness-*.json`` artifact reports a
   678	    ``fail`` status, else None. This keeps staging_blocked honest: it cannot be
   679	    claimed without an on-disk gate block."""
   680	    audit_dir = plan_dir / "audit"
   681	    if not audit_dir.is_dir():
   682	        return None
   683	    for p in sorted(audit_dir.glob("patch-completeness-*.json")):
   684	        try:
   685	            data = json.loads(p.read_text())
   686	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   687	            continue
   688	        if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get("status") == "fail":
   689	            return f"audit/{p.name}"
   690	    return None
   691	
   692	
   693	def _operator_finish_signoff_reason(
   694	    *, terminal_class: str, evidence: str | None, note: str | None
   695	) -> str:
   696	    """Honest signoff_reason naming the ACTUAL terminal class (F002 acceptance
   697	    #4) — never a stopped_no_progress pretence."""
   698	    memo = CLOSEOUT_MEMO_RELPATH
   699	    if terminal_class == "signed_off_adjacent":
   700	        return (
   701	            "operator_finish (terminal=signed_off_adjacent): the auditor signed "
   702	            "off; a downstream gate blocked the automated finalize. Operator "
   703	            f"accepted the feature as merge-ready. See {memo}."
   704	        )
   705	    if terminal_class == "staging_blocked":
   706	        return (
   707	            "operator_finish (terminal=staging_blocked): blocked by a recorded "
   708	            f"downstream gate ({evidence}). Operator accepted as ready-to-stage. "
   709	            f"See {memo}."
   710	        )
   711	    # operator_verified
   712	    return (
   713	        "operator_finish (terminal=operator_verified): operator verified the "
   714	        f"feature out-of-band — {note}. See {memo}."
   715	    )

   718	def _operator_finish_decision_paragraph(
   719	    *, terminal_class: str, reason: str
   720	) -> str:
   721	    """Memo ## Operator decision paragraph naming the terminal class. Echoes the
   722	    signoff_reason so the memo and envelope agree; explicitly avoids any
   723	    no-progress / breaker:no_progress language (F002 acceptance #4)."""
   724	    return (
   725	        f"This feature was finished under terminal class `{terminal_class}` via "
   726	        f"the operator-finish close path (no re-dispatch, no breaker required). "
   727	        f"{reason} The close wrote the signoff envelope, recorded the terminal "
   728	        f"class in the operator-resolution sidecar, and flipped `features.json` "
   729	        f"`passes: true` for this feature."
   730	    )
   731	
   732	
   733	def run_operator_finish(
   734	    *,
   735	    plan_dir: Path,
   736	    plan_id: str,
   737	    feature_id: str,
   738	    terminal_class: str,
   739	    tier: str,
   740	    agents_in_panel: list[str],
   741	    note: str | None = None,
   742	) -> CloseoutResult:
   743	    """Plan 2026-06-02-002 F002 — first-class operator-finish close for honest
   744	    terminal classes (signed_off_adjacent / staging_blocked / operator_verified).
   745	
   746	    Unlike :func:`run_close_out` (which is bound to the stopped_no_progress
   747	    terminal + ``breaker:no_progress``), this path:
   748	      * does NOT require ``breaker:no_progress`` to be active;
   749	      * applies a per-class eligibility gate so the close cannot be a relabel of
   750	        an unresolved audit (anti-bypass);
   751	      * writes a signoff_reason + memo naming the ACTUAL terminal class;
   752	      * performs no paid agent dispatch (reads on-disk envelopes, writes files).
   753	
   754	    Eligibility per class:
   755	      * ``signed_off_adjacent`` — the latest auditor envelope's verdict must be
   756	        ``signed_off`` (else :class:`CloseoutError`).
   757	      * ``staging_blocked`` — a recorded downstream-gate block
   758	        (``patch-completeness-*.json`` with ``status: fail``) must exist.
   759	      * ``operator_verified`` — a non-empty ``note`` (operator rationale) is
   760	        required.
   761	
   762	    Commit order mirrors :func:`run_close_out` (stage-then-commit): validate +
   763	    build payload in memory, then memo → signoff → resolution sidecar →
   764	    (idempotent breaker clear if somehow active) → features.json flip.
   765	    """
   766	    if terminal_class not in TERMINAL_FINISH_CLASSES:
   767	        raise CloseoutError(
   768	            f"unknown terminal class {terminal_class!r}; valid operator-finish "
   769	            f"classes: {sorted(TERMINAL_FINISH_CLASSES)}. For a "
   770	            f"stopped_no_progress close use the no-progress taxonomy via "
   771	            f"run_close_out."
   772	        )
   773	
   774	    audit_paths = _audit_paths_for_feature(plan_dir, feature_id)
   775	    if not audit_paths:
   776	        raise CloseoutError(
   777	            f"no audit envelopes found for feature {feature_id!r} in "
   778	            f"{plan_dir / 'audit'}. The operator-finish close requires at least "
   779	            f"one volley round so the signoff envelope can cite audit history."
   780	        )
   781	
   782	    # Per-class eligibility (anti-bypass).
   783	    evidence: str | None = None
   784	    if terminal_class == "signed_off_adjacent":
   785	        verdict = _latest_auditor_verdict(audit_paths)
   786	        if verdict != "signed_off":
   787	            raise CloseoutError(
   788	                f"signed_off_adjacent refused: the latest auditor verdict is "
   789	                f"{verdict!r}, not 'signed_off'. This terminal class cannot "
   790	                f"relabel a needs_changes / failed audit as operator-resolved. "
   791	                f"Use staging_blocked or operator_verified, or re-dispatch."
   792	            )
   793	    elif terminal_class == "staging_blocked":
   794	        evidence = _recorded_gate_block(plan_dir)
   795	        if evidence is None:
   796	            raise CloseoutError(
   797	                "staging_blocked refused: no recorded downstream-gate block "
   798	                "(expected a patch-completeness-*.json artifact with "
   799	                "status='fail') found under audit/. staging_blocked requires "
   800	                "on-disk gate evidence."
   801	            )
   802	    else:  # operator_verified
   803	        if not (isinstance(note, str) and note.strip()):
   804	            raise CloseoutError(
   805	                "operator_verified refused: a non-empty --note rationale is "
   806	                "required (record what was verified and why the terminal is "
   807	                "non-defect)."
   808	            )
   809	
   810	    signoff_reason = _operator_finish_signoff_reason(
   811	        terminal_class=terminal_class, evidence=evidence, note=note
   812	    )
   813	    decision_paragraph = _operator_finish_decision_paragraph(
   814	        terminal_class=terminal_class, reason=signoff_reason
   815	    )
   816	
   817	    # STAGE: build + validate the signoff payload in memory before any side
   818	    # effect (mirrors run_close_out; an invalid payload raises before disk).
   819	    iteration = max(0, len([p for p in audit_paths if "auditor" in p.name]) - 1)
   820	    payload = signoff_writer.build_signoff_dict(
   821	        plan_id=plan_id,
   822	        tier=tier,
   823	        iteration=iteration,
   824	        agents_in_panel=agents_in_panel,
   825	        audit_paths=audit_paths,
   826	        plan_dir=plan_dir,
   827	        volley_status="signed_off",
   828	        signoff_reason=signoff_reason,
   829	    )
   830	    auditor_envelope = _latest_auditor_envelope(audit_paths)
   831	    memo_body = render_closeout_memo(
   832	        plan_id=plan_id,
   833	        feature_id=feature_id,
   834	        reason_class=terminal_class,
   835	        auditor_envelope=auditor_envelope,
   836	        status_label="operator_finished",
   837	        decision_paragraph=decision_paragraph,
   838	    )
   839	    resolution_payload = _build_operator_resolution_sidecar(
   840	        plan_id=plan_id, feature_id=feature_id, reason_class=terminal_class
   841	    )
   842	    resolution_payload["terminal_class"] = terminal_class
   843	    if note is not None:
   844	        resolution_payload["note"] = note
   845	    if evidence is not None:
   846	        resolution_payload["evidence"] = evidence
   847	
   848	    memo_path = plan_dir / CLOSEOUT_MEMO_RELPATH
   849	    signoff_path = signoff_writer.signoff_path(plan_dir, plan_id)
   850	    resolution_path = operator_resolution_path(plan_dir, plan_id)
   851	
   852	    # COMMIT memo → signoff → resolution sidecar (roll back on OSError).
   853	    memo_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   854	    memo_path.write_text(memo_body)
   855	    signoff_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   856	    try:
   857	        signoff_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
   858	    except OSError:
   859	        memo_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   860	        raise
   861	    try:
   862	        resolution_path.write_text(
   863	            json.dumps(resolution_payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
   864	        )
   865	    except OSError:
   866	        signoff_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   867	        memo_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   868	        raise
   869	
   870	    # Idempotent breaker clear: operator-finish does NOT require the breaker, but
   871	    # if a no_progress breaker happens to be active, clear it so the plan's
   872	    # gate-state doesn't keep blocking dispatch after the feature is finished.
   873	    breaker_gate = circuit_breakers.gate_name(circuit_breakers.BreakerKind.NO_PROGRESS)
   874	    breaker_cleared = False
   875	    if breaker_gate in set(gate_pause.active_breakers(plan_dir)):
   876	        breaker_cleared = gate_pause.approve_gate(
   877	            plan_dir, breaker_gate, plan_id=plan_id, actor="operator"
   878	        )
   879	
   880	    features_json_path, features_changed = _flip_feature_passes(

   899	def format_no_progress_close_hint(
   900	    *, plan_id: str, feature_id: str, recommended_class: str | None
   901	) -> str:
   902	    """Render the operator-facing hint block appended to the
   903	    ``no_progress_classification`` INBOX event. Recommended class is the
   904	    aggregate taxonomy class from auditor_taxonomy (passed by the
   905	    supervisor); ``None`` falls back to a placeholder."""
   906	    class_token = recommended_class or "<class>"
   907	    return (
   908	        "\n\n"
   909	        "To close-out this feature without a re-dispatch (operator accepts "
   910	        "the finding as non-defect):\n"
   911	        f"  dontpanic close --operator-resolved "
   912	        f"{plan_id} {feature_id} --reason {class_token}\n"
   913	        "\n"
   914	        "This generates a closeout-memo template at "
   915	        f"{CLOSEOUT_MEMO_RELPATH}, clears breaker:no_progress, writes the "
   916	        "signoff envelope, and flips features.json passes:true — all in one "
   917	        "transaction."
   918	    )

   200	def write_signoff(
   201	    *,
   202	    plan_id: str,
   203	    tier: str,
   204	    iteration: int,
   205	    agents_in_panel: list[str],
   206	    audit_paths: list[Path],
   207	    plan_dir: Path,
   208	    volley_status: str,
   209	    signoff_reason: str | None = None,
   210	    child_charter: Any = None,
   211	    commit_policy: Any = None,
   212	    modified_files: list[str] | None = None,
   213	    orchestration: Any = None,
   214	    patch_completeness: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   215	) -> Path:
   216	    """Build, validate, and write the signoff.json closeout. Returns the path.
   217	
   218	    Plan 2026-05-02-003 F002: when both ``child_charter`` and ``commit_policy``
   219	    are passed, runs ``check_child_charter_compliance`` BEFORE persisting any
   220	    artifact. On ``ChildCharterViolation`` neither the signoff envelope nor
   221	    the side-car compliance file is written (no-partial-artifact, mirrors
   222	    plan 005 F002 acceptance #5). On compliance pass, writes the side-car
   223	    file at ``audit/charter-compliance-{plan_id}.json`` recording the parsed
   224	    return-condition status + satisfied requires items.
   225	
   226	    The Signoff schema (agent-conventions v1.0) is unchanged — compliance
   227	    output lives in the side-car, matching F001's pop-orchestration-block
   228	    schema-discipline pattern (D006-style).
   229	    """
   230	    payload = build_signoff_dict(
   231	        plan_id=plan_id,
   232	        tier=tier,
   233	        iteration=iteration,
   234	        agents_in_panel=agents_in_panel,
   235	        audit_paths=audit_paths,
   236	        plan_dir=plan_dir,
   237	        volley_status=volley_status,
   238	        signoff_reason=signoff_reason,
   239	        patch_completeness=patch_completeness,
   240	    )
   241	
   242	    compliance: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   243	    if child_charter is not None and commit_policy is not None:
   244	        # Late import to avoid a load-time cycle (nested_orchestration imports
   245	        # nothing from signoff_writer, but plan_loader imports both).
   246	        from dontpanic_orchestrate.nested_orchestration import (
   247	            check_child_charter_compliance,
   248	        )
   249	
   250	        compliance = check_child_charter_compliance(
   251	            plan_dir=plan_dir,
   252	            child_charter=child_charter,
   253	            commit_policy=commit_policy,
   254	            signoff_data=payload,
   255	            modified_files=modified_files,
   256	        )
   257	
   258	    out = signoff_path(plan_dir, plan_id)
   259	    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   260	    out.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

   422	def _build_operator_signoff(
   423	    *,
   424	    plan_id: str,
   425	    feature_id: str,
   426	    reason_class: str,
   427	    audit_paths: list[Path],
   428	    plan_dir: Path,
   429	    tier: str,
   430	    agents_in_panel: list[str],
   431	    iteration: int,
   432	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   433	    """Wrap :func:`signoff_writer.build_signoff_dict` with operator-resolved
   434	    semantics: ``signoff: true`` (operator accepted the feature),
   435	    ``next_action: merge``, and a ``signoff_reason`` that names the class so
   436	    a downstream reader can tell apart operator close-out from auditor
   437	    signoff at a glance."""
   438	    if not audit_paths:
   439	        # Synthesize a placeholder audit path so signoff schema validation
   440	        # passes. We point at the closeout memo (relative URI), which the
   441	        # schema accepts as a path-string.
   442	        raise CloseoutError(
   443	            "no audit envelopes found for feature — cannot write signoff "
   444	            "envelope. Run at least one volley round first or hand-author."
   445	        )
   446	
   447	    signoff_reason = (
   448	        f"operator_resolved (class={reason_class}): operator accepted the "
   449	        f"stopped_no_progress terminal as non-defect after review. "
   450	        f"See {CLOSEOUT_MEMO_RELPATH} for rationale."
   451	    )
   452	    # build_signoff_dict consults volley_status for the next_action mapping.
   453	    # We pass "signed_off" so next_action resolves to "merge" — matches the
   454	    # operator-resolved semantics (feature is accepted, ready to merge).
   455	    payload = signoff_writer.build_signoff_dict(
   456	        plan_id=plan_id,
   457	        tier=tier,
   458	        iteration=iteration,
   459	        agents_in_panel=agents_in_panel,
   460	        audit_paths=audit_paths,
   461	        plan_dir=plan_dir,
   462	        volley_status="signed_off",
   463	        signoff_reason=signoff_reason,
   464	    )
   465	    return payload
   466	
   467	
   468	def _build_operator_resolution_sidecar(
   469	    *,
   470	    plan_id: str,
   471	    feature_id: str,
   472	    reason_class: str,
   473	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   474	    """v3 F004 i1 fix: operator_resolution sidecar payload.
   475	
   476	    Separate file so the Signoff envelope stays schema-clean (extra='forbid').
   477	    Same pattern as charter-compliance-{plan_id}.json. Plan_id is implicit
   478	    in the file path; feature_id + class + memo path are the only fields
   479	    downstream consumers need to tell apart operator-resolved from auditor-
   480	    signed-off close-out.
   481	    """
   482	    return {
   483	        "plan_id": plan_id,
   484	        "feature_id": feature_id,
   485	        "class": reason_class,
   486	        "memo": str(CLOSEOUT_MEMO_RELPATH),
   487	        "resolved": True,
   488	        "resolved_at": _now_iso(),
   489	    }
   490	
   491	
   492	def run_close_out(
   493	    *,
   494	    plan_dir: Path,
   495	    plan_id: str,
   496	    feature_id: str,
   497	    reason_class: str,
   498	    tier: str,
   499	    agents_in_panel: list[str],
   500	    require_active_breaker: bool = True,
   501	) -> CloseoutResult:
   502	    """Execute the close-out workflow end-to-end. Raises
   503	    :class:`CloseoutError` on any safety check failure (unknown class,
   504	    missing feature, no audit history, missing breaker — when
   505	    ``require_active_breaker`` is True).
   506	
   507	    Side effects, in order (stage-then-commit; see module docstring):
   508	      1. Validate inputs (class, feature exists, audit history present).
   509	      2. Build + validate the signoff payload in memory (pure — raises
   510	         :class:`signoff_writer.SignoffWriteError` on validation drift
   511	         without touching disk).
   512	      3. Write ``evidence/closeout-memo.md`` template (mkdir as needed).
   513	      4. Write the operator-resolved signoff envelope.
   514	      5. Clear ``breaker:no_progress`` (idempotent — no-op if not active
   515	         and ``require_active_breaker`` is False). Comes AFTER the signoff
   516	         envelope is durable so a crash here never produces a
   517	         cleared-breaker-without-signoff state (high finding from v3 F004
   518	         i0 audit).
   519	      6. Flip ``features.json`` ``passes: true`` + append a memo
   520	         evidence ref.
   521	    """
   522	    if reason_class not in KNOWN_CLOSEOUT_CLASSES:
   523	        raise CloseoutError(
   524	            f"unknown reason class {reason_class!r}; valid classes: "
   525	            f"{sorted(KNOWN_CLOSEOUT_CLASSES)}. Use the v0 7-class "
   526	            f"taxonomy or 'operator_judgment' for free-form."
   527	        )
   528	
   529	    audit_paths = _audit_paths_for_feature(plan_dir, feature_id)
   530	    if not audit_paths:
   531	        raise CloseoutError(
   532	            f"no audit envelopes found for feature {feature_id!r} in "
   533	            f"{plan_dir / 'audit'}. The close-out workflow requires at "
   534	            f"least one volley round so the signoff envelope can cite "
   535	            f"audit history."
   536	        )
   537	
   538	    # Safety check: refuse to close-out a feature whose breaker isn't
   539	    # currently active. Defends against operators close-ing a feature that
   540	    # didn't actually hit no_progress.
   541	    breaker_gate = circuit_breakers.gate_name(circuit_breakers.BreakerKind.NO_PROGRESS)
   542	    active_breakers = set(gate_pause.active_breakers(plan_dir))
   543	    breaker_active = breaker_gate in active_breakers
   544	    if require_active_breaker and not breaker_active:
   545	        raise CloseoutError(
   546	            f"breaker {breaker_gate!r} is not currently active for {plan_id} "
   547	            f"(active breakers: {sorted(active_breakers) or '[]'}). The "
   548	            f"close-out workflow only runs when the no_progress breaker "
   549	            f"tripped; manually clear the breaker first if you want to "
   550	            f"close-out without the safety check."
   551	        )
   552	
   553	    auditor_envelope = _latest_auditor_envelope(audit_paths)
   554	
   555	    # Step 2 — STAGE: build + validate the signoff payload in memory before
   556	    # any side effect. _build_operator_signoff calls signoff_writer's
   557	    # Pydantic validator; an invalid payload raises SignoffWriteError BEFORE
   558	    # the memo or any state file is touched. (v3 F004 i0 high finding.)
   559	    iteration = max(
   560	        0,
   561	        len([p for p in audit_paths if "auditor" in p.name]) - 1,
   562	    )
   563	    payload = _build_operator_signoff(
   564	        plan_id=plan_id,
   565	        feature_id=feature_id,
   566	        reason_class=reason_class,
   567	        audit_paths=audit_paths,
   568	        plan_dir=plan_dir,
   569	        tier=tier,
   570	        agents_in_panel=agents_in_panel,
   571	        iteration=iteration,
   572	    )
   573	    memo_body = render_closeout_memo(
   574	        plan_id=plan_id,
   575	        feature_id=feature_id,
   576	        reason_class=reason_class,
   577	        auditor_envelope=auditor_envelope,
   578	    )
   579	    signoff_path = signoff_writer.signoff_path(plan_dir, plan_id)
   580	    memo_path = plan_dir / CLOSEOUT_MEMO_RELPATH
   581	    resolution_path = operator_resolution_path(plan_dir, plan_id)
   582	    resolution_payload = _build_operator_resolution_sidecar(
   583	        plan_id=plan_id,
   584	        feature_id=feature_id,
   585	        reason_class=reason_class,
   586	    )
   587	
   588	    # Step 3 — COMMIT closeout memo. Cheapest to roll back if a later step
   589	    # fails (a single unlink). Overwrite if present so re-runs get a fresh
   590	    # template.
   591	    memo_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   592	    memo_path.write_text(memo_body)
   593	
   594	    # Step 4 — COMMIT signoff envelope. Durable record of the operator
   595	    # decision; MUST land on disk before the breaker is cleared so a crash
   596	    # between these two writes never leaves a cleared-breaker-without-
   597	    # signoff state. On OSError, roll back the memo so the operator's next
   598	    # invocation is not staring at a stale template with no signoff peer.
   599	    signoff_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   600	    try:
   601	        signoff_path.write_text(
   602	            json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
   603	        )
   604	    except OSError:
   605	        memo_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   606	        raise
   607	
   608	    # Step 4b — COMMIT operator_resolution sidecar (v3 F004 i1 fix). The
   609	    # Signoff schema has extra='forbid' so this metadata cannot ride along
   610	    # in the validated envelope; instead it lives in audit/operator-
   611	    # resolution-{plan_id}.json (same idiom as charter-compliance-{plan_id}).
   612	    # Rollback both signoff + memo on failure so the operator never sees a
   613	    # half-written closeout state.
   614	    try:
   615	        resolution_path.write_text(
   616	            json.dumps(resolution_payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
   617	        )
   618	    except OSError:
   619	        signoff_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   620	        memo_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   621	        raise
   622	
   623	    # Step 5 — COMMIT breaker clearance. approve_gate returns False when the
   624	    # breaker isn't active; treat that as a non-error (require_active_breaker
   625	    # is False here when the safety check is disabled).
   626	    breaker_cleared = False
   627	    if breaker_active:
   628	        try:
   629	            breaker_cleared = gate_pause.approve_gate(
   630	                plan_dir,
   631	                breaker_gate,
   632	                plan_id=plan_id,
   633	                actor="operator",
   634	            )
   635	        except Exception:
   636	            # Signoff is the durable artifact and is intentionally LEFT in
   637	            # place — re-running close --operator-resolved is idempotent and
   638	            # will re-attempt the breaker clear. We only roll back the memo
   639	            # so the operator doesn't see a stale rationale section.
   640	            raise
   641	
   642	    # Step 6 — COMMIT features.json passes flip + evidence ref. Last in
   643	    # the chain; a failure here leaves the breaker cleared + signoff
   644	    # written. Re-run with --allow-missing-breaker recovers.
   645	    features_json_path, features_changed = _flip_feature_passes(
   646	        plan_dir,
   647	        feature_id,
   648	        reason_class=reason_class,
   649	        memo_relpath=str(CLOSEOUT_MEMO_RELPATH),
   650	    )
   651	
   652	    return CloseoutResult(
   653	        plan_id=plan_id,
   654	        feature_id=feature_id,
   655	        reason_class=reason_class,
   656	        memo_path=memo_path,
   657	        signoff_path=signoff_path,

    65	def _write_auditor(ad: Path, iteration: int, *, status: str, n_findings: int,
    66	                   issue_offset: int = 0) -> Path:
    67	    """Auditor envelope with ``n_findings`` distinct-signature findings starting
    68	    at ``issue_offset``. A shrinking set with the same offset is a strict subset
    69	    (e.g. offset 0, n=1 ⊂ offset 0, n=3)."""
    70	    p = ad / f"codex-auditor-i{iteration}.json"
    71	    p.write_text(json.dumps({
    72	        "task_id": "t", "audit_id": f"t#codex#{iteration}", "agent": "codex",
    73	        "agent_role": "auditor", "iteration": iteration,
    74	        "started_at": _iso(), "completed_at": _iso(), "audit_status": status,
    75	        "findings": [
    76	            {"severity": "high", "category": "correctness",
    77	             "issue": f"finding {i + issue_offset}-aaaaaaa"}
    78	            for i in range(n_findings)
    79	        ],
    80	    }))
    81	    return p

    87	def test_dr_does_not_trip_when_findings_shrink_strict_subset() -> None:
    88	    """THE convergence case: findings 3 → 1 (a strict subset, finding #0 persists).
    89	    Pre-fix DR trips because the intersection {0} is non-empty. Post-fix: strictly
    90	    shrinking count = progress → no trip."""
    91	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
    92	        ad = Path(td)
    93	        _write_auditor(ad, 0, status="needs_changes", n_findings=3)
    94	        _write_auditor(ad, 1, status="needs_changes", n_findings=1)
    95	        tripped, reason = cb.check_diminishing_returns(sorted(ad.glob("*.json")))
    96	        assert not tripped, f"shrinking finding set must NOT trip diminishing_returns; reason={reason!r}"

   618	    def test_distinct_findings_churn_terminates_at_cap(self) -> None:
   619	        """D003 supersede (Plan 2026-06-02-002 F001, operator-confirmed
   620	        2026-06-02) of this test's pre-D003 contract. Both rounds:
   621	        blocked-without-worktree-changes + auditor needs_changes, with a DISTINCT
   622	        finding signature each round.
   623	
   624	        Pre-D003 this tripped `stopped_no_progress` (the test used distinct
   625	        findings to keep DR quiet and isolate the status-identity no-progress
   626	        trip). Under D003 a round whose findings completely turn over is PROGRESS
   627	        — each prior signature is resolved — so neither no_progress nor DR trips,
   628	        and the volley walks off the end at the iteration cap. This is the
   629	        operator-named safety trade: distinct-finding churn is bounded by
   630	        max_iterations, not no_progress. The volley STILL terminates.
   631	
   632	        no_progress's own status-identity coverage now lives on the legacy
   633	        verdict-string path — see
   634	        TestPriorAudStatusCarryOver.test_timeout_with_work_round_does_not_advance_baseline
   635	        and test_f001_convergence_progress.test_no_progress_fallback_*.
   636	        """
   637	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   638	            plan_dir = _make_test_plan(Path(td))
   639	            _disable_quota_gate()
   640	            _patch_run_round_to_force_envelope(
   641	                impl_audit_status="blocked",
   642	                impl_worktree_marker="false",
   643	                aud_audit_status="needs_changes",
   644	                aud_findings_per_iter=[
   645	                    [
   646	                        {
   647	                            "severity": "high",
   648	                            "category": "correctness",
   649	                            "feature_id": "F001",
   650	                            "issue": "round-0 distinct issue text",
   651	                            "evidence": "evidence-0",
   652	                        }
   653	                    ],
   654	                    [
   655	                        {
   656	                            "severity": "high",
   657	                            "category": "correctness",
   658	                            "feature_id": "F001",
   659	                            "issue": "round-1 distinct issue text",
   660	                            "evidence": "evidence-1",
   661	                        }
   662	                    ],
   663	                    [
   664	                        {
   665	                            "severity": "high",
   666	                            "category": "correctness",
   667	                            "feature_id": "F001",
   668	                            "issue": "round-2 distinct issue text",
   669	                            "evidence": "evidence-2",
   670	                        }
   671	                    ],
   672	                ],
   673	            )
   674	            try:
   675	                _register_scripted()
   676	                result = supervisor.dispatch_volley(plan_dir, "F001", max_iterations=2)
   677	            finally:
   678	                _restore_supervisor()
   679	
   680	            assert result.final_status == "stopped_cap", (
   681	                f"distinct-finding churn is progress under D003 (no early "
   682	                f"no_progress stop); it terminates at the iteration cap, got "
   683	                f"{result.final_status!r} reason={result.reason!r}"
   684	            )
   685	
   686	
   687	# ──────────────  prior_aud_status carry-over (audit-focus item 1)  ──────────────
   688	
   689	
   690	class TestPriorAudStatusCarryOver:
   691	    """Audit-focus item 1 — a skipped timeout-with-work round must NOT establish
   692	    the no-progress baseline for the next round. Encoded by walking
   693	    iteration-0 = timeout-with-work, iteration-1 = legitimate
   694	    blocked-without-work, iteration-2 = same. Without the carry-over fix, the
   695	    no-progress baseline would be set by iter-0 and trip on iter-1; with the
   696	    fix, iter-1 establishes the baseline and iter-2 trips, both legitimate
   697	    countable rounds."""
   698	
   699	    def test_timeout_with_work_round_does_not_advance_baseline(self) -> None:
   700	        """When iteration 0 is timeout-with-work and iterations 1 & 2 are
   701	        legitimate, no-progress trips on the *second* legitimate round, not
   702	        the first — proving the timeout-with-work round did not set the
   703	        baseline.
   704	
   705	        D003-preserve (Plan 2026-06-02-002 F001): this test must keep no_progress
   706	        ITSELF as the trip to demonstrate the prior_aud_status carry-over
   707	        mechanic. Under D003 the old "distinct findings each round" technique no
   708	        longer trips no_progress (distinct findings = progress). So the auditor
   709	        findings here carry NO usable issue text (empty ``issue``) — that makes
   710	        ``compute_audit_finding_signature`` return None, the D003 signature
   711	        carve-out is skipped, and no_progress falls back to verdict-string
   712	        equality (the path D003 explicitly preserves). Finding COUNTS strictly
   713	        decrease across the two legitimate rounds (iter1=2 → iter2=1) so the
   714	        diminishing-returns count-fallback stays quiet and no_progress remains the
   715	        isolated trip — exactly the isolation the pre-D003 distinct-signature
   716	        technique provided."""
   717	        # Custom patch wrapping `_run_round` per-iteration: iter 0 = TWW, iters
   718	        # 1+ = legitimate.
   719	        orig = supervisor._run_round
   720	        seen_iters: list[int] = []
   721	
   722	        def wrapped(*args, **kwargs):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
   723	            path = orig(*args, **kwargs)
   724	            role = kwargs.get("role")
   725	            i = kwargs.get("iteration", 0)
   726	            data = json.loads(path.read_text())
   727	            if role == "implementer":
   728	                seen_iters.append(i)
   729	                data["audit_status"] = "blocked"
   730	                markers = list(data.get("validation_performed") or [])
   731	                markers = [m for m in markers if not m.startswith("worktree_changed=")]
   732	                # iter 0 = timeout-with-work; subsequent iterations = legitimate.
   733	                marker = "true" if i == 0 else "false"
   734	                markers.append(f"worktree_changed={marker}")
   735	                markers.append("subprocess_timeout_seconds=600")
   736	                data["validation_performed"] = markers
   737	            elif role == "auditor":
   738	                data["audit_status"] = "needs_changes"
   739	                # D003-preserve: empty issue text → unsigned findings →
   740	                # compute_audit_finding_signature returns None → no_progress
   741	                # falls back to verdict-string equality (the preserved path).
   742	                # Counts strictly DECREASE across the legitimate rounds
   743	                # (iter1=2 → iter2=1) so the diminishing-returns count-fallback
   744	                # stays quiet and no_progress is the isolated trip. iter0 is
   745	                # timeout-with-work, excluded from both detectors, so its count
   746	                # is irrelevant.
   747	                n = 2 if i == 1 else 1
   748	                data["findings"] = [
   749	                    {
   750	                        "severity": "high",
   751	                        "category": "correctness",
   752	                        "feature_id": "F001",
   753	                        "issue": "",
   754	                        "evidence": f"evidence-{i}-{k}",
   755	                    }
   756	                    for k in range(n)
   757	                ]
   758	            path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2) + "\n")
   759	            return path
   760	
   761	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   762	            plan_dir = _make_test_plan(Path(td))
   763	            _disable_quota_gate()
   764	            supervisor._run_round = wrapped
   765	            try:
   766	                _register_scripted()
   767	                # max_iterations=2 → loop runs iters 0, 1, 2 (range(cap+1)).
   768	                # iter 0 TWW → no-progress skipped, baseline NOT advanced.
   769	                # iter 1 legit → no-progress sees prior=None → no trip; baseline = needs_changes.
   770	                # iter 2 legit → no-progress sees prior=needs_changes → trip!
   771	                result = supervisor.dispatch_volley(plan_dir, "F001", max_iterations=2)
   772	            finally:
   773	                _restore_supervisor()
   774

   776	                f"Expected stopped_no_progress on iter 2 (after iter-1 baseline), "
   777	                f"got {result.final_status!r} reason={result.reason!r} "
   778	                f"impl iters seen: {seen_iters}"
   779	            )
   780	            # And specifically: the trip must be on iteration 3 (i.e., the
   781	            # third countable round), confirming the TWW round did not
   782	            # contribute to the count. result.rounds is iteration+1 at trip.
   783	            assert result.rounds == 3, (
   784	                f"Expected trip after 3 rounds total (TWW + 2 legit), got rounds={result.rounds}"
   785	            )

   228	def test_complete_turnover_findings_run_as_progress(capsys) -> None:
   229	    """D003 supersede (Plan 2026-06-02-002 F001, operator-confirmed 2026-06-02)
   230	    of 2026-05-04-003 F003 AC#6 + 2026-05-30-002 F001's count carve-out.
   231	
   232	    A volley whose findings COMPLETELY TURN OVER each round (same count, fully
   233	    distinct issue text, verdict held at needs_changes) is now treated as
   234	    PROGRESS — each round resolves the prior round's blocking findings and
   235	    exposes new ones. The pre-D003 rule treated 'same count, different findings'
   236	    as no-progress and early-stopped at round 2; that is now WRONG for
   237	    audit-driven convergence. New rule: no_progress trips ONLY when no prior
   238	    blocking-finding signature is resolved. Complete-turnover churn does NOT
   239	    early-stop; it is bounded instead by diminishing_returns / max_iterations.
   240	
   241	    Here rounds 0/1/2 carry distinct finding sets (offset 0/100/200) — each is
   242	    progress, so no breaker trips at round 2. The forced script then repeats its
   243	    last envelope (offset 200) for every subsequent round, so rounds 2 & 3 carry
   244	    IDENTICAL signatures → diminishing_returns trips at round 4. The volley still
   245	    terminates; only the early no_progress stop is gone.
   246	
   247	    Subsumption note (flagged for the batched codex audit): under D003,
   248	    stopped_no_progress is unreachable for sound-signature envelopes — the
   249	    nothing-resolved condition is a strict subset of diminishing_returns, which
   250	    the supervisor checks first. no_progress remains reachable only via the
   251	    legacy verdict-string fallback (see test_no_progress_fallback_* in
   252	    test_f001_convergence_progress.py)."""
   253	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   254	        plan_dir = _make_plan(Path(td))
   255	        _register_scripted()
   256	        _disable_quota_gate()
   257	        # max_iterations=3 → iters 0..3 (range(cap+1)). Script offsets 0/100/200
   258	        # are distinct (progress); the 4th round repeats script[-1] (offset 200),
   259	        # so iters 2 & 3 carry identical signatures → diminishing_returns @ round 4.
   260	        _patch_force_auditor_envelopes([
   261	            ("needs_changes", _findings(2, issue_offset=0)),
   262	            ("needs_changes", _findings(2, issue_offset=100)),
   263	            ("needs_changes", _findings(2, issue_offset=200)),
   264	        ])
   265	
   266	        result = supervisor.dispatch_volley(plan_dir, "F001", max_iterations=3)
   267	
   268	        print(f"\n[D003] complete-turnover volley → {result.final_status} @ round {result.rounds}")
   269	        assert result.final_status == "stopped_diminishing_returns", (
   270	            f"complete-turnover churn must NOT early-stop at round 2 (D003); it "
   271	            f"terminates via diminishing_returns once a finding set repeats, got "
   272	            f"{result.final_status}"
   273	        )
   274	        assert result.rounds == 4, (
   275	            f"distinct rounds 0-2 are progress (no early stop); DR trips at the "
   276	            f"first repeated set (round 4), got {result.rounds}"
   277	        )

   166	# ──────────────────────────────  signature  ──────────────────────────────
   167	
   168	
   169	_WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile(r"\s+")
   170	
   171	
   172	def compute_finding_signature(finding_code: str, finding_class: str, issue: str) -> str:
   173	    """Deterministic SHA-256 hex prefix of `{code}|{class}|{normalized_issue}`.
   174	
   175	    Normalization collapses runs of whitespace + lowercases — same finding
   176	    text with different formatting yields the same signature (D001).
   177	    Returns the first 16 hex characters of the digest (64 bits) — enough
   178	    for collision-free identity within a plan tree without bloating the
   179	    parent_chain walk.
   180	    """
   181	    normalized = _WHITESPACE_RE.sub(" ", (issue or "").strip()).lower()
   182	    payload = f"{finding_code}|{finding_class}|{normalized}".encode()
   183	    return hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()[:16]
   184	
   185	
   186	def compute_audit_finding_signature(finding: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
   187	    """Plan 2026-05-02-004: derive a stable signature for a single auditor
   188	    Finding dict (entries inside an audit envelope's ``findings`` list).
   189	
   190	    Composition: ``{file}:{line}`` locality (when ``file`` is set) +
   191	    ``category`` + normalized ``issue`` text, hashed via the existing
   192	    ``compute_finding_signature`` primitive. Severity is intentionally
   193	    excluded (D002) — a high→medium downgrade is the same finding with a
   194	    re-evaluated severity, not a different finding.
   195	
   196	    Returns ``None`` when ``issue`` text is missing or whitespace-only.
   197	    Callers should treat ``None`` as a fallback signal; the diminishing-
   198	    returns breaker degrades to count-based behavior (D004) when ANY round
   199	    in its convergence window has an unsigned finding.
   200	
   201	    Used by ``circuit_breakers.check_diminishing_returns`` to detect 'same
   202	    unresolved findings persist' as opposed to 'auditor flagged a similar
   203	    count of different findings.' Shared with the F001 repeated-finding
   204	    guard via the same primitive so the two surfaces have one notion of
   205	    finding identity.
   206	    """
   207	    issue = finding.get("issue")
   208	    if not isinstance(issue, str) or not issue.strip():
   209	        return None
   210	    file_loc = finding.get("file") or ""
   211	    line_loc = finding.get("line")
   212	    locality = f"{file_loc}:{line_loc}" if file_loc else ""
   213	    category = str(finding.get("category") or "")
   214	    return compute_finding_signature(
   215	        finding_code=locality,
   216	        finding_class=category,
   217	        issue=issue,
   218	    )

   400	def check(git_state: dict, repo_root: Path, touched_files: set[str]) -> CompletenessReport:
   401	    """Pure-function patch-completeness analyzer. See module docstring for spec."""
   402	    findings: list[Finding] = []
   403	    findings.extend(_check_imports(git_state, repo_root, touched_files))
   404	    findings.extend(_check_test_file_missing(git_state))
   405	    findings.extend(_check_generated_artifact_staged(git_state))
   406	    findings.extend(_check_unstaged_dirty(git_state))
   407	
   408	    # Deduplicated union of all files cited in findings, preserving first-seen order.
   409	    seen: set[str] = set()
   410	    files: list[str] = []
   411	    for f in findings:
   412	        for path in f.files:
   413	            if path not in seen:
   414	                seen.add(path)
   415	                files.append(path)
   416	
   417	    return CompletenessReport(
   418	        schema_version=SCHEMA_VERSION,
   419	        status=compute_status(findings),
   420	        findings=findings,
   421	        files=files,
   422	    )

    34	def _write(root: Path, rel: str, body: str = "") -> None:
    35	    p = root / rel
    36	    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    37	    p.write_text(body)

    40	def _git_state(*, untracked=None, unstaged=None, staged=None) -> dict:
    41	    return {
    42	        "untracked": list(untracked or []),
    43	        "unstaged_modified": [{"path": p} for p in (unstaged or [])],

    49	    """mode -> set(files) for the import-detection modes."""
    50	    return {
    51	        f.mode: set(f.files)
    52	        for f in report.findings
    53	        if f.mode in ("source_imports_uncommitted", "test_imports_uncommitted")
    54	    }

    91	            }
    92	            for fid in feature_ids
    93	        ],
    94	    }
    95	    (plan_dir / "plan.md").write_text(_PLAN_TEMPLATE.format(plan_id=plan_id))
    96	    (plan_dir / "features.json").write_text(
    97	        json.dumps(features, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
    98	    )
    99	    return plan_dir
   100	
   101	
   102	def _write_audit_envelope(
   103	    plan_dir: Path,
   104	    *,
   105	    vendor: str = "codex",
   106	    role: str = "auditor",
   107	    feature_id: str = "F001",
   108	    iteration: int = 0,
   109	    audit_status: str,
   110	    findings: list[dict] | None = None,
   111	    summary: str = "synthetic auditor envelope",
   112	) -> Path:
   113	    audit_dir = plan_dir / "audit"
   114	    audit_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   115	    path = audit_dir / f"{vendor}-{role}-{feature_id}-i{iteration}.json"
   116	    path.write_text(
   117	        json.dumps(
   118	            {
   119	                "task_id": plan_dir.name,
   120	                "audit_id": f"{plan_dir.name}#{vendor}#{iteration}",
   121	                "agent": vendor,
   122	                "agent_role": role,
   123	                "iteration": iteration,
   124	                "started_at": "2026-06-02T00:00:00Z",
   125	                "completed_at": "2026-06-02T00:00:00Z",
   126	                "audit_status": audit_status,
   127	                "validation_performed": [f"reviewed i{iteration}"],
   128	                "summary": summary,
   129	                "findings": findings or [],
   130	                "quota_consumed": {"api_calls": 1},
   131	            },
   132	            indent=2,
   133	        )
   134	        + "\n"
   135	    )
   136	    return path
   137	
   138	
   139	def _write_round(plan_dir: Path, *, auditor_status: str, feature_id: str = "F001") -> None:
   140	    """Write a minimal implementer + auditor envelope pair so the close path has
   141	    audit history to cite."""
   142	    _write_audit_envelope(
   143	        plan_dir, vendor="claude", role="implementer", feature_id=feature_id,
   144	        iteration=0, audit_status="signed_off", summary="impl",
   145	    )
   146	    _write_audit_envelope(
   147	        plan_dir, vendor="codex", role="auditor", feature_id=feature_id,
   148	        iteration=0, audit_status=auditor_status,
   149	        findings=[] if auditor_status == "signed_off" else [
   150	            {"severity": "high", "category": "correctness",
   151	             "issue": "blocking finding still open", "evidence": "e"}
   152	        ],
   153	    )
   154	
   155	
   156	def _write_patch_completeness_fail(plan_dir: Path, *, iteration: int = 0) -> Path:
   157	    """Record a downstream-gate block: a patch-completeness artifact with
   158	    status 'fail' (an untracked module would import-fail on a fresh clone)."""
   159	    audit_dir = plan_dir / "audit"
   160	    audit_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   161	    out = audit_dir / f"patch-completeness-{iteration}.json"
   162	    out.write_text(
   163	        json.dumps(
   164	            {
   165	                "schema_version": "1.0",
   166	                "status": "fail",
   167	                "findings": [
   168	                    {
   169	                        "mode": "source_imports_untracked_module",
   170	                        "path": "scripts/dontpanic_orchestrate/plan_review/sizing_gate.py",
   171	                        "reason": "untracked module imported by a changed file",
   172	                    }
   173	                ],
   174	                "files": ["scripts/dontpanic_orchestrate/plan_review/sizing_gate.py"],
   175	            },
   176	            indent=2,
   177	        )
   178	        + "\n"
   179	    )
   180	    return out
   181	
   182	
   183	_PANEL = ["claude", "codex"]

   188	
   189	class TestAntiBypass:
   190	    def test_signed_off_adjacent_refuses_when_latest_verdict_needs_changes(self) -> None:
   191	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   192	            plan_dir = _make_plan(Path(td), "2026-06-02-002-fixture-antibypass")
   193	            _write_round(plan_dir, auditor_status="needs_changes")
   194	            with pytest.raises(closeout.CloseoutError) as exc:
   195	                closeout.run_operator_finish(
   196	                    plan_dir=plan_dir,
   197	                    plan_id=plan_dir.name,
   198	                    feature_id="F001",
   199	                    terminal_class="signed_off_adjacent",
   200	                    tier="trivial",
   201	                    agents_in_panel=_PANEL,
   202	                )
   203	            assert "signed_off" in str(exc.value).lower()
   204	            # Nothing committed: features.json untouched, no signoff envelope.
   205	            data = json.loads((plan_dir / "features.json").read_text())
   206	            assert data["features"][0]["passes"] is False
   207	            assert not (plan_dir / "audit" / f"signoff-{plan_dir.name}.json").exists()
   208	
   209	    def test_cli_refuses_signed_off_adjacent_on_needs_changes(self) -> None:
   210	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   211	            repo = Path(td)
   212	            plan_dir = _make_plan(repo, "2026-06-02-002-infra-fixture-antibypass-cli")
   213	            _write_round(plan_dir, auditor_status="needs_changes")
   214	            out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
   215	            with redirect_stdout(out), redirect_stderr(err):
   216	                rc = cli.main(
   217	                    ["close", "--operator-resolved", str(plan_dir), "F001",
   218	                     "--reason", "signed_off_adjacent"]
   219	                )
   220	            assert rc != 0, f"CLI must refuse; stdout={out.getvalue()}"
   221	            assert "signed_off" in err.getvalue().lower()

   331	def _flip_feature_passes(

   333	    feature_id: str,
   334	    *,
   335	    reason_class: str,
   336	    memo_relpath: str,
   337	    extra_refs: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
   338	) -> tuple[Path, bool]:
   339	    """Set ``passes: true`` for the matching feature in ``features.json``.
   340	
   341	    Returns (path, changed) so callers can report whether the file was
   342	    actually mutated. Idempotent — re-running on an already-flipped feature
   343	    is a no-op. Preserves any pre-existing ``evidence_refs`` and appends a
   344	    new entry pointing at the closeout memo (de-duplicated by uri).
   345	
   346	    ``extra_refs`` lets callers append additional evidence refs (each a dict
   347	    with at least a ``uri``) alongside the memo ref — e.g. the no-paid
   348	    finalizer cites the signed_off auditor envelope so the flip points at the
   349	    artifact that authorized it (F007 AC9). De-duplicated by uri like the memo.
   350	    """
   351	    features_json = plan_dir / "features.json"
   352	    if not features_json.is_file():
   353	        raise CloseoutError(f"features.json not found at {features_json}")
   354	    data = json.loads(features_json.read_text())
   355	    features = data.get("features")
   356	    if not isinstance(features, list):
   357	        raise CloseoutError(f"features.json missing a `features` array: {features_json}")
   358	
   359	    target_idx: int | None = None
   360	    for i, f in enumerate(features):
   361	        if isinstance(f, dict) and f.get("id") == feature_id:
   362	            target_idx = i
   363	            break
   364	    if target_idx is None:
   365	        raise CloseoutError(
   366	            f"feature {feature_id!r} not found in {features_json} "
   367	            f"(available: {[f.get('id') for f in features if isinstance(f, dict)]})"
   368	        )
   369	
   370	    feature = features[target_idx]
   371	    changed = False
   372	    if not feature.get("passes"):
   373	        feature["passes"] = True
   374	        changed = True
   375	
   376	    # Append the closeout-memo evidence ref unless already present.
   377	    refs = feature.get("evidence_refs") or []
   378	    if not isinstance(refs, list):
   379	        refs = []
   380	    memo_uri = memo_relpath

  1404	        # Plan 2026-05-01-004 F003: pre-signoff patch-completeness gate.
  1405	        # Fires ONLY on the signed_off terminal (the supervisor would
  1406	        # otherwise flip passes:true). Other terminals (blocked, paused,

  1432	                iteration=iteration,
  1433	                agents_in_panel=agents_in_panel,

  1435	                plan_dir=plan_dir,
  1436	                volley_status=result.final_status,
  1437	                signoff_reason=result.reason,

  1439	                # signoff-time compliance (allowed_paths + return-condition +
  1440	                # requires) runs before the envelope persists. Top-level
  1441	                # plans (None/None) skip the check entirely.

  1450	        except (

  1452	            nested_orchestration.ChildCharterViolation,
  1453	        ) as exc:

  1455	    return result

   136	def _audit_paths_for_feature(plan_dir: Path, feature_id: str) -> list[Path]:
   137	    """Return the audit envelope paths for ``feature_id``, in append order
   138	    (implementer-i0, auditor-i0, implementer-i1, auditor-i1, ...).
   139	
   140	    Files in ``<plan_dir>/audit/`` follow the
   141	    ``<vendor>-<role>-<feature_id>-i<N>.json`` convention written by
   142	    :mod:`audit_writer`. Patches like ``patch-completeness-*.json`` and
   143	    ``gate-state.json`` are skipped.
   144	    """
   145	    audit_dir = plan_dir / "audit"
   146	    if not audit_dir.is_dir():
   147	        return []
   148	    matches: list[Path] = []
   149	    for p in sorted(audit_dir.iterdir()):
   150	        name = p.name
   151	        if not name.endswith(".json"):
   152	            continue
   153	        if not p.is_file():
   154	            continue
   155	        # File naming: <vendor>-<role>-<feature_id>-i<N>.json. The
   156	        # feature_id segment must match exactly so F001 doesn't pick up
   157	        # F010 envelopes.
   158	        parts = name[:-5].split("-")
   159	        if len(parts) < 4:
   160	            continue
   161	        if feature_id not in parts:
   162	            continue
   163	        # Role must be implementer or auditor — skip patch-completeness etc.
   164	        if not any(p_part in {"implementer", "auditor"} for p_part in parts):
   165	            continue
   166	        matches.append(p)
   167	
   168	    # Sort by (iteration, role-precedence) so implementer-iN precedes auditor-iN.
   169	    def _sort_key(path: Path) -> tuple[int, int]:
   170	        stem = path.name[:-5]
   171	        # iteration suffix: last segment is "i<N>".
   172	        iter_seg = stem.rsplit("-", 1)[-1]
   173	        iteration = int(iter_seg[1:]) if iter_seg.startswith("i") and iter_seg[1:].isdigit() else 0
   174	        # implementer (0) before auditor (1).
   175	        role_rank = 0 if "implementer" in stem else 1 if "auditor" in stem else 2
   176	        return (iteration, role_rank)
   177	
   178	    matches.sort(key=_sort_key)
   179	    return matches
   180	
   181	
   182	def _auditor_iteration_of(path: Path) -> int:
   183	    """Parse the ``i<N>`` iteration suffix from an audit envelope filename.
   184	    Returns 0 when the suffix is absent or malformed."""
   185	    stem = path.name[:-5] if path.name.endswith(".json") else path.name
   186	    iter_seg = stem.rsplit("-", 1)[-1]
   187	    return int(iter_seg[1:]) if iter_seg.startswith("i") and iter_seg[1:].isdigit() else 0
   188

   190	def _latest_auditor_envelope(audit_paths: list[Path]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   191	    """Return the parsed JSON of the freshest auditor envelope, or None when no
   192	    auditor envelope is present.
   193	
   194	    Plan 2026-05-30-002 F002 (D028 fix): rank by modification time (freshest
   195	    wins), with the iteration index as the tie-break. Re-dispatching a feature
   196	    reuses the ``<vendor>-auditor-<FNNN>-iN.json`` filename per iteration, so a
   197	    superseded run with more iterations can leave a STALE higher-index envelope
   198	    on disk. Selecting by iteration index alone (the old ``reversed()`` walk)
   199	    let that stale ``i1 needs_changes`` outrank a fresh ``i0 signed_off``,
   200	    mislabeling ``latest_audit_status`` and refusing a valid finalize.
   201	
   202	    The common cases are unchanged: a single envelope returns itself; a
   203	    monotonic run (i0,i1,i2 written in order, so mtime increases with the
   204	    index) still resolves to the highest index. The iteration tie-break keeps
   205	    same-mtime (coarse-filesystem / same-second) writes ranked by index, so a

 succeeded in 0ms:
   120	    return refs
   121	
   122	
   123	def _promote_mode5(
   124	    report: CompletenessReport,
   125	    *,
   126	    touched_files: set[str],
   127	    unrelated_dirty_state_note: str | None,
   128	) -> tuple[CompletenessReport, list[str]]:
   129	    """F003 owns the Mode 5 escalation. F002 emits info regardless of touched
   130	    set; F003 escalates to block when files fall outside ``touched_files``
   131	    AND no operator note was supplied.
   132	
   133	    Returns ``(promoted_report, unrelated_dirty_files)`` so callers can
   134	    record the cited file list in the signoff block.
   135	    """
   136	    new_findings: list[Finding] = []
   137	    unrelated_dirty: list[str] = []
   138	    for f in report.findings:
   139	        if f.mode != "unstaged_dirty_state":
   140	            new_findings.append(f)
   141	            continue
   142	        outside = sorted(set(f.files) - set(touched_files))
   143	        if outside:
   144	            unrelated_dirty.extend(outside)
   145	        if outside and not unrelated_dirty_state_note:
   146	            promoted = Finding(
   147	                mode=f.mode,
   148	                files=list(f.files),
   149	                reason=(f.reason + " Files outside touched_files: " + ",".join(outside)),
   150	                severity="block",
   151	                recommendation=(
   152	                    "Run: git add -u <paths> for files that should ride along; "
   153	                    "OR pass --unrelated-dirty-state-note <reason> at dispatch."
   154	                ),
   155	            )
   156	            new_findings.append(promoted)
   157	        else:
   158	            new_findings.append(f)
   159	    promoted_report = CompletenessReport(
   160	        schema_version=report.schema_version,
   161	        status=patch_completeness.compute_status(new_findings),
   162	        findings=new_findings,
   163	        files=list(report.files),
   164	    )
   165	    return promoted_report, unrelated_dirty
   166	
   167	
   168	def _persist_report(plan_dir: Path, *, iteration: int, report: CompletenessReport) -> Path:
   169	    audit_dir = plan_dir / "audit"
   170	    audit_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   171	    out = audit_dir / f"patch-completeness-{iteration}.json"
   172	    out.write_text(json.dumps(report.to_dict(), indent=2) + "\n")
   173	    return out
   174	
   175	
   176	def _build_signoff_block(
   177	    promoted_report: CompletenessReport,
   178	    *,
   179	    allow_incomplete_patch_reason: str | None,
   180	    unrelated_dirty_state_note: str | None,
   181	    unrelated_dirty_files: list[str],
   182	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   183	    block: dict[str, Any] = {
   184	        "status": promoted_report.status,
   185	        "findings": [f.to_dict() for f in promoted_report.findings],
   186	        "files": list(promoted_report.files),
   187	    }
   188	    if allow_incomplete_patch_reason is not None:
   189	        block["override_flag"] = "allow_incomplete_patch"
   190	        block["override_reason"] = allow_incomplete_patch_reason
   191	    if unrelated_dirty_state_note is not None:
   192	        block["unrelated_dirty_state_note"] = unrelated_dirty_state_note
   193	        block["unrelated_dirty_files"] = sorted(set(unrelated_dirty_files))
   194	    return block
   195	
   196	
   197	def enforce(
   198	    plan_dir: Path,
   199	    *,
   200	    plan_id: str,
   201	    iteration: int,
   202	    role: str,
   203	    audit_paths: list[Path],
   204	    affected_paths: list[str] | None,
   205	    repo_root: Path,
   206	    allow_incomplete_patch_reason: str | None = None,
   207	    unrelated_dirty_state_note: str | None = None,
   208	    dry_run: bool = False,
   209	    git_state_override: dict | None = None,
   210	) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   211	    """Run F002's analyzer against the F001 sidecar and decide signoff fate.
   212	
   213	    Returns the signoff ``patch_completeness`` block to attach when the gate
   214	    passes (or when overridden); returns ``None`` when no F001 sidecar is
   215	    present and no ``git_state_override`` was supplied (gate is best-effort
   216	    and silently no-ops without git state, same contract as F001 itself).
   217	
   218	    The ``git_state_override`` parameter is used by the CLI strict-dry-run
   219	    preview path: no audit envelope has been finalized yet, so no F001
   220	    sidecar exists, but the operator wants the gate's findings rendered
   221	    against a fresh ``git_state.capture()`` of the current working tree.
   222	
   223	    Raises ``PatchCompletenessError`` only when ALL of the following hold:
   224	      - ``dry_run`` is False
   225	      - report status is ``'fail'`` after Mode 5 promotion
   226	      - ``allow_incomplete_patch_reason`` is None
   227	    """
   228	    # Layer-B re-validation (defense-in-depth). Argparse already validated
   229	    # at layer A; this catches programmatic callers.
   230	    validate_reason(allow_incomplete_patch_reason, flag="--allow-incomplete-patch")
   231	    validate_reason(unrelated_dirty_state_note, flag="--unrelated-dirty-state-note")
   232	
   233	    git_state = git_state_override
   234	    if git_state is None:
   235	        git_state = _load_git_state(plan_dir, iteration=iteration, role=role)
   236	    if git_state is None:
   237	        return None
   238	
   239	    touched_files = _evidence_refs_from_envelopes(audit_paths) | set(affected_paths or [])
   240	    raw_report = patch_completeness.check(git_state, repo_root, touched_files)
   241	    promoted_report, unrelated_dirty_files = _promote_mode5(
   242	        raw_report,
   243	        touched_files=touched_files,
   244	        unrelated_dirty_state_note=unrelated_dirty_state_note,
   245	    )
   246	
   247	    report_path = _persist_report(plan_dir, iteration=iteration, report=promoted_report)
   248	
   249	    block = _build_signoff_block(
   250	        promoted_report,
   251	        allow_incomplete_patch_reason=allow_incomplete_patch_reason,
   252	        unrelated_dirty_state_note=unrelated_dirty_state_note,
   253	        unrelated_dirty_files=unrelated_dirty_files,
   254	    )
   255	
   256	    if dry_run:
   257	        return block
   258	
   259	    if promoted_report.status == "fail" and allow_incomplete_patch_reason is None:
   260	        try:
   261	            inbox.append_event(
   262	                plan_dir,
   263	                event="breaker:patch_incomplete",
   264	                plan_id=plan_id,
   265	                body=PatchCompletenessError._render(promoted_report),
   266	                report_path=str(report_path),
   267	            )
   268	        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover — INBOX write is best-effort  # noqa: S110  # INBOX write is documented best-effort; pragma:no cover above
   269	            pass
   270	        raise PatchCompletenessError(promoted_report, plan_dir)
   271	
   272	    return block

**Feature Verdicts**

F001: `sound`  
D003 inversion is coherent. Complete-turnover churn does not create an infinite loop: truly distinct churn reaches `stopped_cap`, and repeated/persistent findings are caught by `stopped_diminishing_returns` because supervisor checks DR first. The reconciled tests are honest contract updates, not regression cover.

F002: `sound`  
`run_operator_finish` is separate from `run_close_out`. `signed_off_adjacent` requires latest auditor verdict `signed_off`; `staging_blocked` requires a recorded `patch-completeness-*.json` fail; `operator_verified` requires a non-empty note. I found no paid dispatch path.

F003: `needs_changes`  
Finding: [patch_completeness.py](/Users/bayesian/Documents/GitHub/DontPanic/scripts/dontpanic_orchestrate/patch_completeness.py:259) suffix matching treats any prefix as a source root. A dirty path like `docs/generated/dontpanic_orchestrate/plan_review/sizing_gate.py` matches imported candidate `dontpanic_orchestrate/plan_review/sizing_gate.py`, even though it is not importable from the intended source root. That can false-positive an unrelated untracked file via the suffix path. The fix should constrain suffix matches to known source roots such as `scripts/`, or derive import roots explicitly.

**Verification**

I ran `git log --oneline -3` and `git diff HEAD~3 HEAD`. Focused pytest could not run because the sandbox has no usable writable temp directory.

Overall verdict: `needs_changes` due to F003 false-positive overreach.

D003 one-line answer: the inversion plus DR subsumption is correct and safe; `stopped_no_progress` being subsumed for signed findings is an acceptable signal loss because termination remains bounded by DR or max iterations.

**Feature Verdicts**

F001: `sound`  
D003 inversion is coherent. Complete-turnover churn does not create an infinite loop: truly distinct churn reaches `stopped_cap`, and repeated/persistent findings are caught by `stopped_diminishing_returns` because supervisor checks DR first. F003: `needs_changes`

A dirty path like `docs/generated/dontpanic_orchestrate/plan_review/sizing_gate.py` matches imported candidate `dontpanic_orchestrate/plan_review/sizing_gate.py`, even though it is not importable from the intended source root.
